        /**
         * Crear registro de toque S/R
         * @param {Object} candle
         * @param {Object} context
         * @param {number} level
         * @returns {Object}
         */
        _createSRTouchRecord(candle, context, level) {
            return {
                id:        `SRT_${Date.now()}`,
                timestamp: candle.timestamp || Date.now(),
                hora:      TimeUtils.fmt(candle.timestamp || Date.now()),
                simbolo:   candle.symbol    || '',
                level:     MathUtils.round(level, 5),
                tipo:      level > candle.close ? 'RESISTENCIA' : 'SOPORTE',
                close:     candle.close,
                distancia: MathUtils.round(Math.abs(candle.close - level), 5),
                signalId:  context.signalId || null,
                resultado: 'PENDIENTE',
            };
        },

        /**
         * Clasificar tipo de doji
         * @param {Object} candle
         * @param {Object} context
         * @returns {string}
         */
        _classifyDoji(candle, context) {
            const wicks  = MathUtils.calculateWicks(
                candle.open, candle.high, candle.low, candle.close
            );
            const body   = MathUtils.calculateBody(
                candle.open, candle.close, candle.high, candle.low
            );

            // Doji estándar: mechas casi iguales
            if (Math.abs(wicks.upper - wicks.lower) < 10 && body < 10) {
                return 'DOJI_ESTANDAR';
            }
            // Dragonfly: mecha inferior larga, superior pequeña
            if (wicks.lower > 30 && wicks.upper < 10) {
                return 'DOJI_DRAGONFLY';
            }
            // Gravestone: mecha superior larga, inferior pequeña
            if (wicks.upper > 30 && wicks.lower < 10) {
                return 'DOJI_GRAVESTONE';
            }
            // Long-legged: ambas mechas largas
            if (wicks.upper > 20 && wicks.lower > 20) {
                return 'DOJI_LONG_LEGGED';
            }
            return 'DOJI_OTRO';
        },

        /**
         * Clasificar tipo de marubozu
         * @param {Object} candle
         * @param {Object} context
         * @returns {string}
         */
        _classifyMarubozu(candle, context) {
            const esAlcista = candle.close > candle.open;
            const body      = MathUtils.calculateBody(
                candle.open, candle.close, candle.high, candle.low
            );

            if (body >= 90) {
                return esAlcista ? 'MARUBOZU_BULL_PERFECTO' : 'MARUBOZU_BEAR_PERFECTO';
            }
            if (body >= 70) {
                return esAlcista ? 'MARUBOZU_BULL_FUERTE' : 'MARUBOZU_BEAR_FUERTE';
            }
            return esAlcista ? 'MARUBOZU_BULL_MODERADO' : 'MARUBOZU_BEAR_MODERADO';
        },

        /**
         * Contar toques históricos de un nivel S/R
         * @param {number} level
         * @returns {number}
         */
        _countSRTouches(level) {
            const tol = CFG.DOJI_NIVEL_CLAVE_DIST * 2;
            return this.srTouches.filter(t =>
                Math.abs(t.level - level) <= tol
            ).length;
        },

        /**
         * Calcular niveles S/R desde velas (wrapper interno)
         * @param {Object[]} velas
         * @returns {Object}
         */
        _calculateSRLevels(velas) {
            return PatternDetector.detectSupportResistance(velas);
        },

        /**
         * Recortar datos si excede límites
         */
        _trimData() {
            const maxSize = Math.floor(CFG.MAX_PATTERNS / 2);

            if (this.dojiData.length > maxSize) {
                this.dojiData = this.dojiData.slice(-maxSize);
            }
            if (this.marubozuData.length > maxSize) {
                this.marubozuData = this.marubozuData.slice(-maxSize);
            }
            if (this.srTouches.length > maxSize) {
                this.srTouches = this.srTouches.slice(-maxSize);
            }
        },
    };

    // ════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
    // [BLOQUE 7.3] StatisticalValidator — VALIDACIÓN Z-SCORE
    // ════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════

    const StatisticalValidator = {

        // ─── MÉTODOS PÚBLICOS ────────────────────────────────────────────

        /**
         * Verificar si un win rate es estadísticamente significativo
         * @param {number} wins       - Número de victorias
         * @param {number} total      - Total de señales
         * @param {number} confidence - Nivel de confianza (0.90, 0.95, 0.99)
         * @returns {boolean}
         */
        isSignificant(wins, total, confidence = 0.95) {
            try {
                if (!this._validateInputs(wins, total)) return false;

                const winRate = wins / total;
                const zScore  = this.calculateZScore(winRate, 0.5, total);
                const zCrit   = this._getZValue(confidence);

                return Math.abs(zScore) >= zCrit;
            } catch (e) {
                return false;
            }
        },

        /**
         * Calcular Z-Score de un win rate vs tasa esperada
         * @param {number} winRate      - Win rate observado (0-1)
         * @param {number} expectedRate - Tasa esperada (default: 0.5)
         * @param {number} total        - Tamaño de muestra
         * @returns {number}
         */
        calculateZScore(winRate, expectedRate = 0.5, total) {
            try {
                if (!this._validateInputs(Math.round(winRate * total), total)) return 0;

                const se = this._calculateStandardError(expectedRate, total);
                if (se === 0) return 0;

                return MathUtils.round((winRate - expectedRate) / se, 4);
            } catch (e) {
                return 0;
            }
        },

        /**
         * Calcular intervalo de confianza para un win rate
         * @param {number} wins
         * @param {number} total
         * @param {number} confidence
         * @returns {{ lower: number, upper: number, margin: number }}
         */
        calculateConfidenceInterval(wins, total, confidence = 0.95) {
            const empty = { lower: 0, upper: 1, margin: 0.5 };
            try {
                if (!this._validateInputs(wins, total)) return empty;

                const winRate = wins / total;
                const zCrit   = this._getZValue(confidence);
                const se      = this._calculateStandardError(winRate, total);
                const margin  = zCrit * se;

                return {
                    lower:  MathUtils.round(Math.max(0, winRate - margin), 4),
                    upper:  MathUtils.round(Math.min(1, winRate + margin), 4),
                    margin: MathUtils.round(margin, 4),
                };
            } catch (e) {
                return empty;
            }
        },

        /**
         * Calcular tamaño mínimo de muestra para detectar un efecto
         * @param {number} effectSize  - Diferencia mínima detectable (ej: 0.10 = 10%)
         * @param {number} confidence  - Nivel de confianza
         * @returns {number}
         */
        getMinimumSampleSize(effectSize = 0.10, confidence = 0.95) {
            try {
                const zCrit = this._getZValue(confidence);
                // Fórmula: n = (z * σ / efectSize)² donde σ = 0.5 (máxima varianza)
                const sigma = 0.5;
                const n     = Math.pow((zCrit * sigma) / effectSize, 2);
                return Math.ceil(n);
            } catch (e) {
                return CFG.LEARN_MIN_SIGNALS;
            }
        },

        /**
         * Evaluar calidad estadística de un conjunto de señales
         * @param {number} wins
         * @param {number} total
         * @returns {Object}
         */
        evaluate(wins, total) {
            if (!this._validateInputs(wins, total)) {
                return {
                    valid:      false,
                    winRate:    0,
                    zScore:     0,
                    significant:false,
                    ci95:       { lower: 0, upper: 1, margin: 0.5 },
                    quality:    'INSUFICIENTE',
                };
            }

            const winRate   = MathUtils.round(wins / total, 4);
            const zScore    = this.calculateZScore(winRate, 0.5, total);
            const signif    = this.isSignificant(wins, total, 0.95);
            const ci        = this.calculateConfidenceInterval(wins, total, 0.95);

            let quality = 'INSUFICIENTE';
            if (total >= 50  && signif && winRate > 0.60) quality = 'EXCELENTE';
            else if (total >= 30 && signif && winRate > 0.55) quality = 'BUENA';
            else if (total >= 15 && signif) quality = 'ACEPTABLE';
            else if (total >= 5)  quality = 'PRELIMINAR';

            return {
                valid:       true,
                winRate,
                zScore,
                significant: signif,
                ci95:        ci,
                quality,
                total,
                wins,
                losses: total - wins,
            };
        },

        // ─── MÉTODOS PRIVADOS ────────────────────────────────────────────

        /**
         * Calcular error estándar (proporción binomial)
         * @param {number} p     - Proporción (win rate)
         * @param {number} total - Tamaño de muestra
         * @returns {number}
         */
        _calculateStandardError(p, total) {
            if (total <= 0) return 0;
            const variance = p * (1 - p);
            return Math.sqrt(variance / total);
        },

        /**
         * Obtener valor Z crítico para un nivel de confianza
         * @param {number} confidence
         * @returns {number}
         */
        _getZValue(confidence) {
            const zTable = {
                0.80: 1.282,
                0.85: 1.440,
                0.90: 1.645,
                0.95: 1.960,
                0.99: 2.576,
            };

            // Buscar el valor exacto o el más cercano
            const exact = zTable[confidence];
            if (exact !== undefined) return exact;

            // Interpolación simple para valores intermedios
            const keys   = Object.keys(zTable).map(Number).sort((a, b) => a - b);
            const lower  = keys.filter(k => k <= confidence).pop();
            const upper  = keys.filter(k => k >= confidence).shift();

            if (!lower && !upper) return 1.960;
            if (!lower) return zTable[upper];
            if (!upper) return zTable[lower];
            if (lower === upper) return zTable[lower];

            // Interpolación lineal
            const ratio = (confidence - lower) / (upper - lower);
            return MathUtils.round(
                zTable[lower] + ratio * (zTable[upper] - zTable[lower]), 3
            );
        },

        /**
         * Validar entradas numéricas
         * @param {number} wins
         * @param {number} total
         * @returns {boolean}
         */
        _validateInputs(wins, total) {
            if (!MathUtils._isValidNumber(wins))  return false;
            if (!MathUtils._isValidNumber(total)) return false;
            if (total <= 0)   return false;
            if (wins < 0)     return false;
            if (wins > total) return false;
            return true;
        },
    };

    // ════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
    // EXPOSICIÓN TEMPORAL — BLOQUE 7
    // ════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════

    window._TCP_AILearning           = AILearning;
    window._TCP_PatternRegistry      = PatternRegistry;
    window._TCP_StatisticalValidator = StatisticalValidator;

    // Cargar patrones guardados al inicio
    AILearning.loadPatterns();

    console.log(`%c👑 TCP PRO v4.3 — PARTE 5 CARGADA
    ✅ [7.1] AILearning           → 6 dimensiones de análisis | Auto cada ${CFG.LEARN_AUTO_EVERY} señales
    ✅ [7.2] PatternRegistry      → Dojis (5 tipos) + Marubozus (6 tipos) + S/R
    ✅ [7.3] StatisticalValidator → Z-Score + IC95% + Tamaño mínimo muestra
    📂 Patrones cargados: ${AILearning._patterns.length} activos`,
        `color:${CFG.THEME_PRIMARY};font-weight:bold;font-size:12px`
    );
        // ════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
    // [BLOQUE 8.1] UI_MainPanel — PANEL PRINCIPAL
    // ════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════

    const UI_MainPanel = {

        // ─── ESTADO ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────
        _created:     false,
        _minimized:   false,
        _dragging:    false,
        _dragOffX:    0,
        _dragOffY:    0,

        // ─── MÉTODOS PÚBLICOS ────────────────────────────────────────────

        /**
         * Crear panel principal en el DOM
         */
        create() {
            if (this._created) return;
            try {
                this._injectCSS();
                const panel = this._createHTML();
                document.body.appendChild(panel);
                this._makeDraggable();
                this._bindEvents();
                this._created = true;
                DOMUtils.register('tcp-main-panel', panel);
                console.log('[UI_MainPanel] ✅ Panel principal creado.');
            } catch (e) {
                console.error('[UI_MainPanel] Error create():', e?.message);
            }
        },

        /**
         * Actualizar todos los elementos del panel
         */
        update() {
            if (!this._created) return;
            try {
                const state = window._TCP_STATE;
                if (!state) return;

                this.updateIndicators(
                    state.ema8Actual,
                    state.ema21Actual,
                    state.ema34Actual,
                    state.rsiActual
                );
                this.updateTrend(state.tendenciaActual);
                this.updateImpulse(state.impulsoActual);
                this.updateFilters(FilterEngine.getActiveFilters());
                this.updateSession(TimeUtils.getSessionTime());

                // OHLC de vela actual
                if (state.velaActual) {
                    this.updateOHLC(state.velaActual);
                }
            } catch (e) { /* Silenciar errores de UI */ }
        },

        /**
         * Actualizar precio actual
         * @param {number} price
         */
        updatePrice(price) {
            if (!MathUtils._isValidNumber(price)) return;
            DOMUtils.updateIfChanged('tcp-price', price.toFixed(5));
        },

        /**
         * Actualizar OHLC de vela actual
         * @param {Object} candle
         */
        updateOHLC(candle) {
            if (!candle) return;
            DOMUtils.updateIfChanged('tcp-open',  candle.open?.toFixed(5)  || '--');
            DOMUtils.updateIfChanged('tcp-high',  candle.high?.toFixed(5)  || '--');
            DOMUtils.updateIfChanged('tcp-low',   candle.low?.toFixed(5)   || '--');
            DOMUtils.updateIfChanged('tcp-close', candle.close?.toFixed(5) || '--');
            DOMUtils.updateIfChanged('tcp-ticks', candle.ticks?.toString() || '0');
        },

        /**
         * Actualizar indicadores técnicos
         * @param {number} ema8
         * @param {number} ema21
         * @param {number} ema34
         * @param {number} rsi
         */
        updateIndicators(ema8, ema21, ema34, rsi) {
            DOMUtils.updateIfChanged('tcp-ema8',  ema8?.toFixed(5)  || '--');
            DOMUtils.updateIfChanged('tcp-ema21', ema21?.toFixed(5) || '--');
            DOMUtils.updateIfChanged('tcp-ema34', ema34?.toFixed(5) || '--');

            if (rsi !== null && rsi !== undefined) {
                DOMUtils.updateIfChanged('tcp-rsi', rsi.toFixed(2));
                const rsiColor = rsi >= 70 ? CFG.THEME_PUT
                    : rsi <= 30           ? CFG.THEME_CALL
                    : CFG.THEME_TEXT_PRIMARY;
                DOMUtils.sc('tcp-rsi', rsiColor);
            } else {
                DOMUtils.updateIfChanged('tcp-rsi', '--');
            }
        },

        /**
         * Actualizar tendencia actual
         * @param {string} trend
         */
        updateTrend(trend) {
            const labels = {
                'ALCISTA': '▲ ALCISTA',
                'BAJISTA': '▼ BAJISTA',
                'LATERAL': '◆ LATERAL',
            };
            const colors = {
                'ALCISTA': CFG.THEME_CALL,
                'BAJISTA': CFG.THEME_PUT,
                'LATERAL': CFG.THEME_TEXT_SECONDARY,
            };
            DOMUtils.updateIfChanged('tcp-trend', labels[trend] || '◆ LATERAL');
            DOMUtils.sc('tcp-trend', colors[trend] || CFG.THEME_TEXT_SECONDARY);
        },

        /**
         * Actualizar score y score adaptativo
         * @param {number} score
         * @param {number} adaptive
         */
        updateScore(score, adaptive) {
            const label = ScoringSystem.getScoreLabel(score);
            const state = ScoringSystem.getScoreState(score);
            const color = score >= CFG.SCORE_MIN     ? CFG.THEME_CALL
                        : score >= CFG.SCORE_OBSERVAR ? CFG.THEME_SECONDARY
                        : CFG.THEME_TEXT_SECONDARY;

            DOMUtils.updateIfChanged('tcp-score',       `${score} ${label}`);
            DOMUtils.updateIfChanged('tcp-score-adapt', adaptive?.toFixed(1) || '--');
            DOMUtils.updateIfChanged('tcp-score-state', state);
            DOMUtils.sc('tcp-score', color);
            DOMUtils.sc('tcp-score-state', color);
        },

        /**
         * Actualizar filtros activos
         * @param {string[]} filters
         */
        updateFilters(filters) {
            const text = filters.length > 0
                ? `🚫 ${filters.join(' | ')}`
                : '✅ Sin filtros activos';
            const color = filters.length > 0
                ? CFG.THEME_PUT
                : CFG.THEME_CALL;
            DOMUtils.updateIfChanged('tcp-filters', text);
            DOMUtils.sc('tcp-filters', color);
        },

        /**
         * Actualizar estado del impulso
         * @param {Object} impulso
         */
        updateImpulse(impulso) {
            if (!impulso) return;
            const dir   = impulso.dir   || '--';
            const cnt   = impulso.conteo || 0;
            const force = impulso.fuerzaAvg?.toFixed(1) || '0';
            const doji  = impulso.dojiCount || 0;
            const anul  = impulso.anulado ? ' [ANULADO]' : '';

            const text  = `${dir} ×${cnt} | F:${force}% | D:${doji}${anul}`;
            const color = impulso.anulado ? CFG.THEME_PUT
                : (dir === 'CALL'        ? CFG.THEME_CALL
                : (dir === 'PUT'         ? CFG.THEME_PUT
                : CFG.THEME_TEXT_SECONDARY));

            DOMUtils.updateIfChanged('tcp-impulse', text);
            DOMUtils.sc('tcp-impulse', color);
        },

        /**
         * Actualizar señal activa en el panel
         * @param {Object} signal
         */
        updateSignal(signal) {
            if (!signal) return;
            const emoji = signal.direccion === 'CALL' ? '🟢' : '🔴';
            const color = signal.direccion === 'CALL' ? CFG.THEME_CALL : CFG.THEME_PUT;

            DOMUtils.updateIfChanged('tcp-signal-dir',   `${emoji} ${signal.direccion}`);
            DOMUtils.updateIfChanged('tcp-signal-score', `Score: ${signal.score} ${signal.scoreLabel}`);
            DOMUtils.updateIfChanged('tcp-signal-time',  signal.timestamp || '--');
            DOMUtils.sc('tcp-signal-dir', color);

            // Flash animation
            DOMUtils.addClass('tcp-signal-box', 'tcp-signal-flash');
            setTimeout(() => DOMUtils.removeClass('tcp-signal-box', 'tcp-signal-flash'), 1500);
        },

        /**
         * Actualizar tiempo de sesión
         * @param {string} time
         */
        updateSession(time) {
            DOMUtils.updateIfChanged('tcp-session-time', time || '--');
        },

        /**
         * Minimizar panel
         */
        minimize() {
            this._minimized = true;
            DOMUtils.hide('tcp-panel-body');
            DOMUtils.updateIfChanged('tcp-minimize-btn', '▲');
        },

        /**
         * Maximizar panel
         */
        maximize() {
            this._minimized = false;
            DOMUtils.show('tcp-panel-body', 'flex');
            DOMUtils.updateIfChanged('tcp-minimize-btn', '▼');
        },

        /**
         * Toggle visibilidad
         */
        toggle() {
            if (this._minimized) this.maximize();
            else this.minimize();
        },

        // ─── MÉTODOS PRIVADOS ────────────────────────────────────────────

        /**
         * Hacer el panel arrastrable
         */
        _makeDraggable() {
            const panel  = DOMUtils.ge('tcp-main-panel');
            const header = DOMUtils.ge('tcp-panel-header');
            if (!panel || !header) return;

            const self = this;

            header.addEventListener('mousedown', (e) => {
                if (e.target.closest('button')) return;
                self._dragging  = true;
                const rect      = panel.getBoundingClientRect();
                self._dragOffX  = e.clientX - rect.left;
                self._dragOffY  = e.clientY - rect.top;
                panel.style.transition = 'none';
            });

            document.addEventListener('mousemove', (e) => {
                if (!self._dragging) return;
                const x = MathUtils.clamp(e.clientX - self._dragOffX, 0,
                    window.innerWidth  - panel.offsetWidth);
                const y = MathUtils.clamp(e.clientY - self._dragOffY, 0,
                    window.innerHeight - panel.offsetHeight);
                panel.style.left = x + 'px';
                panel.style.top  = y + 'px';
            });

            document.addEventListener('mouseup', () => {
                self._dragging = false;
                panel.style.transition = '';
            });
        },

        /**
         * Bind de eventos de botones
         */
        _bindEvents() {
            const minBtn = DOMUtils.ge('tcp-minimize-btn');
            if (minBtn) minBtn.addEventListener('click', () => this.toggle());

            const cfgBtn = DOMUtils.ge('tcp-config-btn');
            if (cfgBtn) cfgBtn.addEventListener('click', () => {
                if (window._TCP_UI_Configuration) {
                    window._TCP_UI_Configuration.toggle();
                }
            });
        },
    };

    // ════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
    // [BLOQUE 8.2] UI_Semaforo — SEMÁFORO SINCRONIZADO
    // ════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════

    const UI_Semaforo = {

        // ─── ESTADO ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────
        _shadowRoot: null,
        _host:       null,
        _created:    false,
        _currentLight: 'off',

        // ─── MÉTODOS PÚBLICOS ────────────────────────────────────────────

        /**
         * Crear semáforo con Shadow DOM aislado
         */
        create() {
            if (this._created) return;
            try {
                this._host       = document.createElement('div');
                this._host.id    = 'tcp-semaforo-host';
                Object.assign(this._host.style, {
                    position: 'fixed',
                    top:      '60px',
                    right:    '10px',
                    zIndex:   '999998',
                });
                document.body.appendChild(this._host);

                // Crear Shadow DOM para aislamiento total de estilos
                this._shadowRoot = this._createShadowDOM();
                this._makeDraggable();
                this._created    = true;
                console.log('[UI_Semaforo] ✅ Semáforo Shadow DOM creado.');
            } catch (e) {
                console.error('[UI_Semaforo] Error create():', e?.message);
            }
        },

        /**
         * Actualizar todos los datos del semáforo
         */
        update() {
            if (!this._created) return;
            try {
                const state = window._TCP_STATE;
                if (!state) return;

                this.updateRSI(state.rsiActual);
                this.updateEMAs(state.ema8Actual, state.ema21Actual, state.ema34Actual);
                this.updateImpulse(state.impulsoActual);

                const conditions = this._calculateConditions();
                this.updateConditions(conditions);
                this._updateLight(conditions);
            } catch (e) { /* Silenciar */ }
        },

        /**
         * Actualizar precio en semáforo
         * @param {number} price
         */
        updatePrice(price) {
            if (!this._shadowRoot) return;
            const el = this._shadowRoot.getElementById('sem-price');
            if (el && MathUtils._isValidNumber(price)) {
                el.textContent = price.toFixed(5);
            }
        },

        /**
         * Actualizar RSI
         * @param {number} rsi
         */
        updateRSI(rsi) {
            if (!this._shadowRoot) return;
            const el  = this._shadowRoot.getElementById('sem-rsi-val');
            const bar = this._shadowRoot.getElementById('sem-rsi-bar');
            if (el) el.textContent = rsi?.toFixed(1) || '--';
            this._updateRSIBar(rsi);
        },

        /**
         * Actualizar EMAs
         * @param {number} ema8
         * @param {number} ema21
         * @param {number} ema34
         */
        updateEMAs(ema8, ema21, ema34) {
            if (!this._shadowRoot) return;
            const set = (id, val) => {
                const el = this._shadowRoot.getElementById(id);
                if (el) el.textContent = val?.toFixed(5) || '--';
            };
            set('sem-ema8',  ema8);
            set('sem-ema21', ema21);
            set('sem-ema34', ema34);
        },

        /**
         * Actualizar impulso en semáforo
         * @param {Object} impulso
         */
        updateImpulse(impulso) {
            if (!this._shadowRoot || !impulso) return;
            const el = this._shadowRoot.getElementById('sem-impulse');
            if (!el) return;

            const dir   = impulso.dir    || '--';
            const cnt   = impulso.conteo || 0;
            const anul  = impulso.anulado ? ' ✗' : '';
            el.textContent = `${dir} ×${cnt}${anul}`;
            el.style.color = impulso.anulado ? '#FF1744'
                : dir === 'CALL'             ? '#00E676'
                : dir === 'PUT'              ? '#FF1744'
                : '#C5C6C7';
        },

        /**
         * Actualizar condiciones (0-5) en semáforo
         * @param {number} conditions
         */
        updateConditions(conditions) {
            if (!this._shadowRoot) return;
            const dots = this._shadowRoot.querySelectorAll('.sem-dot');
            dots.forEach((dot, i) => {
                dot.classList.toggle('sem-dot-active', i < conditions);
            });
            const el = this._shadowRoot.getElementById('sem-cond-count');
            if (el) el.textContent = `${conditions}/5`;
        },

        /**
         * Cambiar luz del semáforo
         * @param {string} color - 'red' | 'yellow' | 'green' | 'off'
         */
        setLight(color) {
            if (!this._shadowRoot) return;
            this._currentLight = color;

            const lights = {
                red:    this._shadowRoot.getElementById('sem-light-red'),
                yellow: this._shadowRoot.getElementById('sem-light-yellow'),
                green:  this._shadowRoot.getElementById('sem-light-green'),
            };

            Object.entries(lights).forEach(([k, el]) => {
                if (!el) return;
                el.classList.toggle('sem-light-on', k === color);
                el.classList.toggle('sem-light-off', k !== color);
            });
        },

        /**
         * Mostrar mensaje de estado
         * @param {string} msg
         */
        showStatus(msg) {
            if (!this._shadowRoot) return;
            const el = this._shadowRoot.getElementById('sem-status');
            if (el) el.textContent = msg || '';
        },

        // ─── MÉTODOS PRIVADOS ────────────────────────────────────────────

        /**
         * Crear Shadow DOM completo del semáforo
         * @returns {ShadowRoot}
         */
        _createShadowDOM() {
            const shadow = this._host.attachShadow({ mode: 'open' });

            shadow.innerHTML = `
                <style>
                    :host { display: block; }
                    * { box-sizing: border-box; font-family: 'Courier New', monospace; }
                    #sem-container {
                        width: 160px;
                        background: ${CFG.THEME_BG_PRIMARY};
                        border: 1px solid ${CFG.THEME_PRIMARY};
                        border-radius: 10px;
                        padding: 8px;
                        box-shadow: 0 0 15px rgba(255,215,0,0.25);
                        user-select: none;
                        cursor: move;
                    }
                    #sem-header {
                        text-align: center;
                        color: ${CFG.THEME_PRIMARY};
                        font-size: 10px;
                        font-weight: bold;
                        margin-bottom: 6px;
                        letter-spacing: 1px;
                        border-bottom: 1px solid ${CFG.THEME_PRIMARY}33;
                        padding-bottom: 4px;
                    }
                    #sem-price {
                        text-align: center;
                        font-size: 13px;
                        font-weight: bold;
                        color: ${CFG.THEME_PRIMARY};
                        margin-bottom: 6px;
                        letter-spacing: 1px;
                    }
                    /* SEMÁFORO */
                    #sem-lights {
                        display: flex;
                        flex-direction: column;
                        align-items: center;
                        gap: 4px;
                        background: #111;
                        border-radius: 8px;
                        padding: 6px;
                        margin-bottom: 6px;
                        border: 1px solid #333;
                    }
                    .sem-light {
                        width: 28px; height: 28px;
                        border-radius: 50%;
                        transition: all 0.3s ease;
                    }
                    .sem-light-off  { opacity: 0.15; filter: none; }
                    .sem-light-on   { opacity: 1; }
                    #sem-light-red    { background: #FF1744; }
                    #sem-light-yellow { background: #FFC107; }
                    #sem-light-green  { background: #00E676; }
                    #sem-light-red.sem-light-on {
                        box-shadow: 0 0 12px #FF1744, 0 0 24px #FF174488;
                    }
                    #sem-light-yellow.sem-light-on {
                        box-shadow: 0 0 12px #FFC107, 0 0 24px #FFC10788;
                    }
                    #sem-light-green.sem-light-on {
                        box-shadow: 0 0 12px #00E676, 0 0 24px #00E67688;
                    }
                    /* CONDICIONES */
                    #sem-conditions {
                        display: flex;
                        justify-content: center;
                        gap: 4px;
                        margin-bottom: 4px;
                    }
                    .sem-dot {
                        width: 10px; height: 10px;
                        border-radius: 50%;
                        background: #333;
                        transition: all 0.3s;
                    }
                    .sem-dot-active { background: ${CFG.THEME_PRIMARY}; }
                    #sem-cond-count {
                        text-align: center;
                        font-size: 10px;
                        color: ${CFG.THEME_TEXT_SECONDARY};
                        margin-bottom: 4px;
                    }
                    /* DATOS */
                    .sem-row {
                        display: flex;
                        justify-content: space-between;
                        font-size: 9px;
                        margin: 2px 0;
                        color: ${CFG.THEME_TEXT_PRIMARY};
                    }
                    .sem-label { color: ${CFG.THEME_TEXT_SECONDARY}; }
                    /* RSI BAR */
                    #sem-rsi-bar-bg {
                        height: 4px;
                        background: #222;
                        border-radius: 2px;
                        margin: 3px 0;
                        position: relative;
                        overflow: hidden;
                    }
                    #sem-rsi-bar {
                        height: 100%;
                        width: 50%;
                        background: ${CFG.THEME_PRIMARY};
                        border-radius: 2px;
                        transition: width 0.3s, background 0.3s;
                    }
                    #sem-impulse {
                        text-align: center;
                        font-size: 10px;
                        font-weight: bold;
                        margin: 3px 0;
                        color: ${CFG.THEME_TEXT_SECONDARY};
                    }
                    #sem-status {
                        text-align: center;
                        font-size: 9px;
                        color: ${CFG.THEME_TEXT_SECONDARY};
                        margin-top: 3px;
                        min-height: 12px;
                    }
                </style>

                <div id="sem-container">
                    <div id="sem-header">🚦 SEMÁFORO TCP</div>
                    <div id="sem-price">--</div>

                    <div id="sem-lights">
                        <div id="sem-light-red"    class="sem-light sem-light-off"></div>
                        <div id="sem-light-yellow" class="sem-light sem-light-off"></div>
                        <div id="sem-light-green"  class="sem-light sem-light-off"></div>
                    </div>

                    <div id="sem-conditions">
                        <div class="sem-dot"></div>
                        <div class="sem-dot"></div>
                        <div class="sem-dot"></div>
                        <div class="sem-dot"></div>
                        <div class="sem-dot"></div>
                    </div>
                    <div id="sem-cond-count">0/5</div>

                    <div class="sem-row">
                        <span class="sem-label">RSI</span>
                        <span id="sem-rsi-val">--</span>
                    </div>
                    <div id="sem-rsi-bar-bg">
                        <div id="sem-rsi-bar"></div>
                    </div>

                    <div class="sem-row">
                        <span class="sem-label">EMA8</span>
                        <span id="sem-ema8">--</span>
                    </div>
                    <div class="sem-row">
                        <span class="sem-label">EMA21</span>
                        <span id="sem-ema21">--</span>
                    </div>
                    <div class="sem-row">
                        <span class="sem-label">EMA34</span>
                        <span id="sem-ema34">--</span>
                    </div>

                    <div id="sem-impulse">-- ×0</div>
                    <div id="sem-status"></div>
                </div>
            `;

            return shadow;
        },

        /**
         * Calcular 5 condiciones para el semáforo
         * @returns {number} 0-5
         */
        _calculateConditions() {
            const state = window._TCP_STATE;
            if (!state) return 0;

            let cond = 0;

            // C1: Tendencia definida
            if (state.tendenciaActual !== 'LATERAL') cond++;

            // C2: RSI en zona operativa
            if (state.rsiActual !== null &&
                state.rsiActual >= CFG.RSI_POST_EXTREMO_MIN &&
                state.rsiActual <= CFG.RSI_POST_EXTREMO_MAX) cond++;

            // C3: EMAs alineadas
            if (state.ema8Actual && state.ema21Actual && state.ema34Actual) {
                const alc = state.ema8Actual > state.ema21Actual &&
                            state.ema21Actual > state.ema34Actual;
                const baj = state.ema8Actual < state.ema21Actual &&
                            state.ema21Actual < state.ema34Actual;
                if (alc || baj) cond++;
            }

            // C4: Impulso activo y no anulado
            if (state.impulsoActual?.conteo >= CFG.IMPULSO_VELAS_MIN &&
                !state.impulsoActual?.anulado) cond++;

            // C5: Sin filtros bloqueantes
            if (FilterEngine.getActiveFilters().length === 0) cond++;

            return cond;
        },

        /**
         * Actualizar luz del semáforo según condiciones
         * @param {number} conditions
         */
        _updateLight(conditions) {
            if (conditions >= 4)      this.setLight('green');
            else if (conditions >= 2) this.setLight('yellow');
            else                      this.setLight('red');
        },

        /**
         * Actualizar barra visual del RSI
         * @param {number} rsi
         */
        _updateRSIBar(rsi) {
            if (!this._shadowRoot) return;
            const bar = this._shadowRoot.getElementById('sem-rsi-bar');
            if (!bar || rsi === null || rsi === undefined) return;

            const pct   = MathUtils.clamp(rsi, 0, 100);
            bar.style.width = pct + '%';
            bar.style.background = rsi >= 70 ? CFG.THEME_PUT
                : rsi <= 30                  ? CFG.THEME_CALL
                : CFG.THEME_PRIMARY;
        },

        /**
         * Formatear condición para display
         * @param {string} cond
         * @param {*}      value
         * @returns {string}
         */
        _formatCondition(cond, value) {
            return `${cond}: ${value}`;
        },

        /**
         * Hacer el semáforo arrastrable
         */
        _makeDraggable() {
            if (!this._shadowRoot || !this._host) return;
            let dragging = false, offX = 0, offY = 0;

            const container = this._shadowRoot.getElementById('sem-container');
            if (!container) return;

            container.addEventListener('mousedown', (e) => {
                dragging = true;
                const rect = this._host.getBoundingClientRect();
                offX = e.clientX - rect.left;
                offY = e.clientY - rect.top;
            });

            document.addEventListener('mousemove', (e) => {
                if (!dragging) return;
                const x = MathUtils.clamp(e.clientX - offX, 0,
                    window.innerWidth  - (this._host.offsetWidth  || 170));
                const y = MathUtils.clamp(e.clientY - offY, 0,
                    window.innerHeight - (this._host.offsetHeight || 300));
                this._host.style.left = x + 'px';
                this._host.style.top  = y + 'px';
                this._host.style.right = 'auto';
            });

            document.addEventListener('mouseup', () => { dragging = false; });
        },

        /**
         * Bind de eventos internos
         */
        _bindEvents() {},
    };

    // ════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
    // [BLOQUE 8.3] UI_MultiSymbol — DASHBOARD MULTI-PAR
    // ════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════

    const UI_MultiSymbol = {

        _created:  false,
        _visible:  true,

        create() {
            if (this._created || !CFG.MULTI_SIMBOLO_ACTIVO) return;
            try {
                this._injectCSS();
                const panel = this._createHTML();
                document.body.appendChild(panel);
                this._bindEvents();
                this._created = true;
                DOMUtils.register('tcp-multi-panel', panel);
                console.log('[UI_MultiSymbol] ✅ Dashboard multi-par creado.');
            } catch (e) {
                console.error('[UI_MultiSymbol] Error create():', e?.message);
            }
        },

        update() {
            if (!this._created) return;
            try {
                const data = window._TCP_MultiSymbolDashboard?.getDashboardData() || [];
                data.forEach(d => this.updateSymbol(d.simbolo, d));
            } catch (e) { /* Silenciar */ }
        },

        updateSymbol(sym, data) {
            if (!sym || !data) return;
            this._updateSymbolCard(sym, data);
        },

        showAlert(sym, conditions) {
            if (!this._created) return;
            const card = DOMUtils.ge(`tcp-card-${sym}`);
            if (card) {
                card.classList.add('tcp-card-alert');
                setTimeout(() => card.classList.remove('tcp-card-alert'), 5000);
            }
            this._createAlertBadge(sym, conditions);
        },

        clearAlert(sym) {
            const badge = DOMUtils.ge(`tcp-alert-${sym}`);
            if (badge) badge.style.display = 'none';
        },

        toggle() {
            if (this._visible) this.hide();
            else this.show();
        },

        show() {
            this._visible = true;
            DOMUtils.show('tcp-multi-panel');
        },

        hide() {
            this._visible = false;
            DOMUtils.hide('tcp-multi-panel');
        },

        // ─── PRIVADOS ────────────────────────────────────────────────────

        _createHTML() {
            const panel     = document.createElement('div');
            panel.id        = 'tcp-multi-panel';
            const cards     = CFG.MULTI_SIMBOLO_PARES
                .filter(p => p.activo)
                .map(p => this._createSymbolCard(p.simbolo, {
                    nombre: p.nombre, conditions: 0, score: 0,
                    tendencia: 'LATERAL', rsi: null, hasAlert: false,
                })).join('');
            panel.innerHTML = `
                <div id="tcp-multi-header">
                    <span>📊 MULTI-PAR</span>
                    <button id="tcp-multi-toggle" class="tcp-btn-icon">▼</button>
                </div>
                <div id="tcp-multi-body">${cards}</div>
            `;
            return panel;
        },

        _createSymbolCard(sym, data) {
            const safeSym = sym.replace(/[^a-zA-Z0-9_]/g, '');
            return `
                <div id="tcp-card-${safeSym}" class="tcp-sym-card">
                    <div class="tcp-sym-name">${data.nombre || sym}</div>
                    <div class="tcp-sym-row">
                        <span class="tcp-sym-label">Cond</span>
                        <span id="tcp-cond-${safeSym}" class="tcp-sym-val">0/5</span>
                    </div>
                    <div class="tcp-sym-row">
                        <span class="tcp-sym-label">RSI</span>
                        <span id="tcp-rsi-${safeSym}" class="tcp-sym-val">--</span>
                    </div>
                    <div class="tcp-sym-row">
                        <span class="tcp-sym-label">Tend</span>
                        <span id="tcp-tend-${safeSym}" class="tcp-sym-val">--</span>
                    </div>
                    <div id="tcp-alert-${safeSym}" class="tcp-sym-alert" style="display:none">
                        🔔 REVISAR
                    </div>
                </div>
            `;
        },

        _updateSymbolCard(sym, data) {
            const safeSym = sym.replace(/[^a-zA-Z0-9_]/g, '');
            DOMUtils.updateIfChanged(`tcp-cond-${safeSym}`,
                `${data.conditions || 0}/5`);
            DOMUtils.updateIfChanged(`tcp-rsi-${safeSym}`,
                data.rsi?.toFixed(1) || '--');
            DOMUtils.updateIfChanged(`tcp-tend-${safeSym}`,
                data.tendencia || '--');

            // Color de condiciones
            const condEl = DOMUtils.ge(`tcp-cond-${safeSym}`);
            if (condEl) {
                condEl.style.color = (data.conditions || 0) >= 4 ? CFG.THEME_CALL
                    : (data.conditions || 0) >= 2              ? CFG.THEME_SECONDARY
                    : CFG.THEME_TEXT_SECONDARY;
            }
        },

        _createAlertBadge(sym, conditions) {
            const safeSym = sym.replace(/[^a-zA-Z0-9_]/g, '');
            const badge   = DOMUtils.ge(`tcp-alert-${safeSym}`);
            if (badge) {
                badge.style.display = 'block';
                badge.textContent   = `🔔 ${conditions}/5 condiciones`;
            }
        },

        _playAlertAnimation(sym) {
            const safeSym = sym.replace(/[^a-zA-Z0-9_]/g, '');
            const card    = DOMUtils.ge(`tcp-card-${safeSym}`);
            if (card) {
                card.classList.add('tcp-card-alert');
                setTimeout(() => card.classList.remove('tcp-card-alert'), 3000);
            }
        },

        _injectCSS() {
            if (document.getElementById('tcp-multi-css')) return;
            const style = document.createElement('style');
            style.id    = 'tcp-multi-css';
            style.textContent = `
                #tcp-multi-panel {
                    position: fixed;
                    bottom: 10px; left: 10px;
                    width: 240px;
                    background: ${CFG.THEME_BG_PRIMARY};
                    border: 1px solid ${CFG.THEME_PRIMARY}88;
                    border-radius: 8px;
                    font-family: 'Courier New', monospace;
                    font-size: 10px;
                    color: ${CFG.THEME_TEXT_PRIMARY};
                    z-index: 999997;
                    box-shadow: 0 0 12px rgba(255,215,0,0.2);
                }
                #tcp-multi-header {
                    display: flex;
                    justify-content: space-between;
                    align-items: center;
                    padding: 6px 10px;
                    background: ${CFG.THEME_BG_SECONDARY};
                    border-radius: 7px 7px 0 0;
                    color: ${CFG.THEME_PRIMARY};
                    font-weight: bold;
                    font-size: 11px;
                }
                #tcp-multi-body {
                    display: grid;
                    grid-template-columns: 1fr 1fr;
                    gap: 4px;
                    padding: 6px;
                }
                .tcp-sym-card {
                    background: ${CFG.THEME_BG_SECONDARY}88;
                    border: 1px solid ${CFG.THEME_PRIMARY}33;
                    border-radius: 6px;
                    padding: 5px;
                    transition: border-color 0.3s;
                }
                .tcp-sym-name {
                    color: ${CFG.THEME_PRIMARY};
                    font-size: 9px;
                    font-weight: bold;
                    margin-bottom: 3px;
                    white-space: nowrap;
                    overflow: hidden;
                    text-overflow: ellipsis;
                }
                .tcp-sym-row {
                    display: flex;
                    justify-content: space-between;
                    margin: 1px 0;
                }
                .tcp-sym-label { color: ${CFG.THEME_TEXT_SECONDARY}; font-size: 9px; }
                .tcp-sym-val   { font-size: 9px; }
                .tcp-sym-alert {
                    margin-top: 3px;
                    background: ${CFG.THEME_PRIMARY}22;
                    border: 1px solid ${CFG.THEME_PRIMARY};
                    border-radius: 3px;
                    text-align: center;
                    font-size: 8px;
                    color: ${CFG.THEME_PRIMARY};
                    padding: 1px;
                    font-weight: bold;
                }
                @keyframes tcp-card-pulse {
                    0%,100% { border-color: ${CFG.THEME_PRIMARY}33; }
                    50%     { border-color: ${CFG.THEME_PRIMARY}; }
                }
                .tcp-card-alert {
                    animation: tcp-card-pulse 0.6s ease-in-out 5;
                }
            `;
            document.head.appendChild(style);
        },

        _bindEvents() {
            const toggle = DOMUtils.ge('tcp-multi-toggle');
            if (toggle) toggle.addEventListener('click', () => {
                const body = DOMUtils.ge('tcp-multi-body');
                if (body) {
                    const hidden = body.style.display === 'none';
                    body.style.display = hidden ? 'grid' : 'none';
                    toggle.textContent = hidden ? '▼' : '▲';
                }
            });
        },
    };

    // ════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
    // [BLOQUE 8.4] UI_Configuration — PANEL DE CONFIGURACIÓN
    // ════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════

    const UI_Configuration = {

        _created: false,
        _visible: false,

        create() {
            if (this._created) return;
            try {
                this._injectCSS();
                const panel = this._createHTML();
                document.body.appendChild(panel);
                this._bindEvents();
                this._created = true;
                DOMUtils.register('tcp-cfg-panel', panel);
                console.log('[UI_Configuration] ✅ Panel de configuración creado.');
            } catch (e) {
                console.error('[UI_Configuration] Error create():', e?.message);
            }
        },

        show() {
            this._visible = true;
            DOMUtils.show('tcp-cfg-panel');
        },

        hide() {
            this._visible = false;
            DOMUtils.hide('tcp-cfg-panel');
        },

        toggle() {
            if (this._visible) this.hide();
            else this.show();
        },

        saveConfig() {
            try {
                const config = {
                    dojiMode:        DOMUtils.ge('cfg-doji-mode')?.value        || CFG.DOJI_MODE,
                    divergenciaMode: DOMUtils.ge('cfg-div-mode')?.value         || CFG.DIVERGENCIA_MODO,
                    scoreMin:    parseInt(DOMUtils.ge('cfg-score-min')?.value)  || CFG.SCORE_MIN,
                    cooldownMs:  parseInt(DOMUtils.ge('cfg-cooldown')?.value)   * 1000 || CFG.COOLDOWN_MS,
                    multiActivo: DOMUtils.ge('cfg-multi-activo')?.checked       ?? CFG.MULTI_SIMBOLO_ACTIVO,
                    notif:       DOMUtils.ge('cfg-notif')?.checked              ?? CFG.MULTI_SIMBOLO_NOTIFICACIONES,
                };
                if (!this._validateConfig(config)) {
                    alert('❌ Configuración inválida. Revisa los valores.');
                    return;
                }
                window._TCP_StorageManager?.saveConfig(config);
                alert('✅ Configuración guardada. Recarga para aplicar cambios.');
            } catch (e) {
                console.error('[UI_Configuration] Error saveConfig:', e?.message);
            }
        },

        loadConfig() {
            try {
                const config = window._TCP_StorageManager?.loadConfig() || {};
                if (config.dojiMode)        { const el = DOMUtils.ge('cfg-doji-mode');    if (el) el.value   = config.dojiMode; }
                if (config.divergenciaMode) { const el = DOMUtils.ge('cfg-div-mode');     if (el) el.value   = config.divergenciaMode; }
                if (config.scoreMin)        { const el = DOMUtils.ge('cfg-score-min');    if (el) el.value   = config.scoreMin; }
                if (config.cooldownMs)      { const el = DOMUtils.ge('cfg-cooldown');     if (el) el.value   = config.cooldownMs / 1000; }
                if (config.multiActivo !== undefined) { const el = DOMUtils.ge('cfg-multi-activo'); if (el) el.checked = config.multiActivo; }
                if (config.notif !== undefined)       { const el = DOMUtils.ge('cfg-notif');        if (el) el.checked = config.notif; }
            } catch (e) {
                console.warn('[UI_Configuration] Error loadConfig:', e?.message);
            }
        },

        resetToDefaults() {
            const inputs = ['cfg-doji-mode','cfg-div-mode','cfg-score-min','cfg-cooldown'];
            inputs.forEach(id => {
                const el = DOMUtils.ge(id);
                if (el) el.value = '';
            });
        },

        // ─── PRIVADOS ────────────────────────────────────────────────────

        _createHTML() {
            const panel   = document.createElement('div');
            panel.id      = 'tcp-cfg-panel';
            panel.style.display = 'none';
            panel.innerHTML = `
                <div id="tcp-cfg-header">
                    ⚙️ CONFIGURACIÓN TCP PRO v4.3
                    <button id="tcp-cfg-close" class="tcp-btn-icon">✕</button>
                </div>
                <div id="tcp-cfg-body">

                    <div class="cfg-group">
                        <label class="cfg-label">Modo Dojis</label>
                        ${this._createDojiModeSelector()}
                    </div>

                    <div class="cfg-group">
                        <label class="cfg-label">Modo Divergencias</label>
                        ${this._createDivergenceModeSelector()}
                    </div>

                    <div class="cfg-group">
                        <label class="cfg-label">Score Mínimo (actual: ${CFG.SCORE_MIN})</label>
                        <input id="cfg-score-min" type="number" class="cfg-input"
                            value="${CFG.SCORE_MIN}" min="5" max="18" step="1">
                    </div>

                    <div class="cfg-group">
                        <label class="cfg-label">Cooldown señal (seg)</label>
                        <input id="cfg-cooldown" type="number" class="cfg-input"
                            value="${CFG.COOLDOWN_MS/1000}" min="10" max="300" step="5">
                    </div>

                    ${this._createMultiSymbolConfig()}

                    <div class="cfg-group">
                        <label class="cfg-label">Notificaciones</label>
                        <input id="cfg-notif" type="checkbox"
                            ${CFG.MULTI_SIMBOLO_NOTIFICACIONES ? 'checked' : ''}>
                    </div>

                    <div class="cfg-actions">
                        <button id="cfg-save-btn"   class="cfg-btn cfg-btn-primary">💾 Guardar</button>
                        <button id="cfg-reset-btn"  class="cfg-btn cfg-btn-secondary">↺ Defaults</button>
                        <button id="cfg-learn-btn"  class="cfg-btn cfg-btn-accent">🧠 Aprender IA</button>
                    </div>

                    <div id="cfg-ai-summary" class="cfg-section-title" style="margin-top:8px">
                        IA: ${AILearning._patterns.length} patrones activos
                    </div>
                </div>
            `;
            return panel;
        },

        _createDojiModeSelector() {
            return `
                <select id="cfg-doji-mode" class="cfg-select">
                    <option value="ORIGINAL"   ${CFG.DOJI_MODE==='ORIGINAL'  ?'selected':''}>ORIGINAL (pausa neutral)</option>
                    <option value="HYBRID"     ${CFG.DOJI_MODE==='HYBRID'    ?'selected':''}>HYBRID (penalización)</option>
                    <option value="CONTEXTUAL" ${CFG.DOJI_MODE==='CONTEXTUAL'?'selected':''}>CONTEXTUAL (confirmación)</option>
                </select>
            `;
        },

        _createDivergenceModeSelector() {
            return `
                <select id="cfg-div-mode" class="cfg-select">
                    <option value="FRACTAL" ${CFG.DIVERGENCIA_MODO==='FRACTAL'?'selected':''}>FRACTAL (3 velas)</option>
                    <option value="VENTANA" ${CFG.DIVERGENCIA_MODO==='VENTANA'?'selected':''}>VENTANA (8-15 velas)</option>
                </select>
            `;
        },

        _createMultiSymbolConfig() {
            return `
                <div class="cfg-group">
                    <label class="cfg-label">Multi-Símbolo Activo</label>
                    <input id="cfg-multi-activo" type="checkbox"
                        ${CFG.MULTI_SIMBOLO_ACTIVO ? 'checked' : ''}>
                </div>
            `;
        },

        _createThresholdInputs() {
            return `
                <div class="cfg-group">
                    <label class="cfg-label">RSI Agotamiento CALL</label>
                    <input type="number" class="cfg-input" value="${CFG.RSI_AGOTAMIENTO_CALL}">
                </div>
            `;
        },

        _injectCSS() {
            if (document.getElementById('tcp-cfg-css')) return;
            const style = document.createElement('style');
            style.id    = 'tcp-cfg-css';
            style.textContent = `
                #tcp-cfg-panel {
                    position: fixed;
                    top: 50%; left: 50%;
                    transform: translate(-50%, -50%);
                    width: 300px;
                    background: ${CFG.THEME_BG_PRIMARY};
                    border: 1px solid ${CFG.THEME_PRIMARY};
                    border-radius: 10px;
                    font-family: 'Courier New', monospace;
                    font-size: 11px;
                    color: ${CFG.THEME_TEXT_PRIMARY};
                    z-index: 1000000;
                    box-shadow: 0 0 30px rgba(255,215,0,0.4);
                }
                #tcp-cfg-header {
                    display: flex;
                    justify-content: space-between;
                    align-items: center;
                    padding: 10px 12px;
                    background: ${CFG.THEME_BG_SECONDARY};
                    border-radius: 9px 9px 0 0;
                    color: ${CFG.THEME_PRIMARY};
                    font-weight: bold;
                    font-size: 12px;
                }
                #tcp-cfg-body {
                    padding: 10px 12px;
                    display: flex;
                    flex-direction: column;
                    gap: 8px;
                    max-height: 70vh;
                    overflow-y: auto;
                }
                .cfg-group {
                    display: flex;
                    flex-direction: column;
                    gap: 3px;
                }
                .cfg-label {
                    color: ${CFG.THEME_TEXT_SECONDARY};
                    font-size: 10px;
                }
                .cfg-input, .cfg-select {
                    background: ${CFG.THEME_BG_SECONDARY};
                    border: 1px solid ${CFG.THEME_PRIMARY}44;
                    border-radius: 4px;
                    color: ${CFG.THEME_TEXT_PRIMARY};
                    padding: 4px 6px;
                    font-family: 'Courier New', monospace;
                    font-size: 11px;
                    outline: none;
                }
                .cfg-input:focus, .cfg-select:focus {
                    border-color: ${CFG.THEME_PRIMARY};
                }
                .cfg-actions {
                    display: flex;
                    gap: 5px;
                    flex-wrap: wrap;
                }
                .cfg-btn {
                    flex: 1;
                    padding: 6px 4px;
                    border: none;
                    border-radius: 5px;
                    cursor: pointer;
                    font-size: 10px;
                    font-family: 'Courier New', monospace;
                    font-weight: bold;
                    transition: opacity 0.2s;
                }
                .cfg-btn:hover { opacity: 0.85; }
                .cfg-btn-primary   { background: ${CFG.THEME_PRIMARY}; color: #000; }
                .cfg-btn-secondary { background: ${CFG.THEME_BG_SECONDARY}; color: ${CFG.THEME_TEXT_PRIMARY}; border: 1px solid ${CFG.THEME_PRIMARY}44; }
                .cfg-btn-accent    { background: #7B2FBE; color: #fff; }
                .cfg-section-title { color: ${CFG.THEME_TEXT_SECONDARY}; font-size: 9px; }
            `;
            document.head.appendChild(style);
        },

        _bindEvents() {
            const close = DOMUtils.ge('tcp-cfg-close');
            if (close) close.addEventListener('click', () => this.hide());

            const save = DOMUtils.ge('cfg-save-btn');
            if (save) save.addEventListener('click', () => this.saveConfig());

            const reset = DOMUtils.ge('cfg-reset-btn');
            if (reset) reset.addEventListener('click', () => this.resetToDefaults());

            const learn = DOMUtils.ge('cfg-learn-btn');
            if (learn) learn.addEventListener('click', () => {
                window._TCP_AILearning?.learn();
                DOMUtils.updateIfChanged('cfg-ai-summary',
                    `IA: ${AILearning._patterns.length} patrones activos`);
            });
        },

        _validateConfig(config) {
            if (!['ORIGINAL','HYBRID','CONTEXTUAL'].includes(config.dojiMode)) return false;
            if (!['FRACTAL','VENTANA'].includes(config.divergenciaMode))       return false;
            if (config.scoreMin < 1 || config.scoreMin > 20)                  return false;
            if (config.cooldownMs < 5000 || config.cooldownMs > 300000)       return false;
            return true;
        },
    };

    // ════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
    // [BLOQUE 8.5] UI_Controls — BOTONES DE ACCIÓN
    // ════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════

    const UI_Controls = {

        _created: false,

        create() {
            if (this._created) return;
            try {
                this._injectCSS();
                const panel = this._createHTML();
                document.body.appendChild(panel);
                this._bindEvents();
                this._created = true;
                DOMUtils.register('tcp-controls-panel', panel);
                console.log('[UI_Controls] ✅ Controles creados.');
            } catch (e) {
                console.error('[UI_Controls] Error create():', e?.message);
            }
        },

        exportSignalsCSV() {
            try {
                const csv = window._TCP_StorageManager?.exportSignals() || '';
                if (!csv) { this._showError('Sin señales para exportar.'); return; }
                this._triggerDownload(csv, `tcp_signals_${TimeUtils.fmtDate()}.csv`);
                this._showSuccess('Señales exportadas.');
            } catch (e) { this._showError('Error al exportar señales.'); }
        },

        exportMarketLogCSV() {
            try {
                const csv = window._TCP_StorageManager?.exportMarketLog() || '';
                if (!csv) { this._showError('Sin datos de mercado para exportar.'); return; }
                this._triggerDownload(csv, `tcp_market_${TimeUtils.fmtDate()}.csv`);
                this._showSuccess('MarketLog exportado.');
            } catch (e) { this._showError('Error al exportar MarketLog.'); }
        },

        clearSignals() {
            if (!this._showConfirmation('¿Borrar todas las señales?')) return;
            window._TCP_StorageManager?.clearSignals();
            this._showSuccess('Señales borradas.');
        },

        clearMarketLog() {
            if (!this._showConfirmation('¿Borrar el MarketLog?')) return;
            window._TCP_MarketLog?.clear();
            this._showSuccess('MarketLog borrado.');
        },

        resetSession() {
            if (!this._showConfirmation('¿Resetear sesión? (mantiene datos)')) return;
            window._TCP_STATE?.resetSession();
            window._TCP_StorageManager?.resetSession();
            this._showSuccess('Sesión reseteada.');
        },

        resetAll() {
            if (!this._showConfirmation(
                '⚠️ RESET TOTAL: Borrará TODOS los datos. ¿Continuar?')) return;
            window._TCP_StorageManager?.resetAll();
            window._TCP_AILearning?.reset();
            this._showSuccess('Reset total completado. Recarga la página.');
        },

        requestNotifications() {
            if (!('Notification' in window)) {
                this._showError('Notificaciones no soportadas.'); return;
            }
            Notification.requestPermission().then(perm => {
                if (perm === 'granted') this._showSuccess('✅ Notificaciones habilitadas.');
                else this._showError('❌ Notificaciones denegadas.');
            });
        },

        // ─── PRIVADOS ────────────────────────────────────────────────────

        _createHTML() {
            const panel   = document.createElement('div');
            panel.id      = 'tcp-controls-panel';
            panel.innerHTML = `
                <div id="tcp-ctrl-header">🎮 CONTROLES TCP PRO</div>
                <div id="tcp-ctrl-body">
                    <div class="ctrl-group">
                        <span class="ctrl-title">📥 Exportar</span>
                        <button id="ctrl-exp-signals" class="ctrl-btn">Señales CSV</button>
                        <button id="ctrl-exp-market"  class="ctrl-btn">MarketLog CSV</button>
                    </div>
                    <div class="ctrl-group">
                        <span class="ctrl-title">🗑️ Borrar</span>
                        <button id="ctrl-clr-signals" class="ctrl-btn ctrl-btn-warn">Borrar Señales</button>
                        <button id="ctrl-clr-market"  class="ctrl-btn ctrl-btn-warn">Borrar MarketLog</button>
                    </div>
                    <div class="ctrl-group">
                        <span class="ctrl-title">🔄 Resetear</span>
                        <button id="ctrl-rst-session" class="ctrl-btn ctrl-btn-warn">Reset Sesión</button>
                        <button id="ctrl-rst-all"     class="ctrl-btn ctrl-btn-danger">Reset Total</button>
                    </div>
                    <div class="ctrl-group">
                        <span class="ctrl-title">🔔 Sistema</span>
                        <button id="ctrl-notif"       class="ctrl-btn">Notificaciones</button>
                    </div>
                    <div id="ctrl-msg" class="ctrl-msg"></div>
                </div>
            `;
            return panel;
        },

        _injectCSS() {
            if (document.getElementById('tcp-ctrl-css')) return;
            const style = document.createElement('style');
            style.id    = 'tcp-ctrl-css';
            style.textContent = `
                #tcp-controls-panel {
                    position: fixed;
                    bottom: 10px; right: 10px;
                    width: 170px;
                    background: ${CFG.THEME_BG_PRIMARY};
                    border: 1px solid ${CFG.THEME_PRIMARY}66;
                    border-radius: 8px;
                    font-family: 'Courier New', monospace;
                    font-size: 10px;
                    color: ${CFG.THEME_TEXT_PRIMARY};
                    z-index: 999996;
                    box-shadow: 0 0 10px rgba(255,215,0,0.15);
                }
                #tcp-ctrl-header {
                    padding: 6px 10px;
                    background: ${CFG.THEME_BG_SECONDARY};
                    border-radius: 7px 7px 0 0;
                    color: ${CFG.THEME_PRIMARY};
                    font-weight: bold;
                    font-size: 10px;
                }
                #tcp-ctrl-body {
                    padding: 6px 8px;
                    display: flex;
                    flex-direction: column;
                    gap: 6px;
                }
                .ctrl-group {
                    display: flex;
                    flex-direction: column;
                    gap: 3px;
                }
                .ctrl-title {
                    color: ${CFG.THEME_TEXT_SECONDARY};
                    font-size: 9px;
                    font-weight: bold;
                }
                .ctrl-btn {
                    padding: 4px 6px;
                    background: ${CFG.THEME_BG_SECONDARY};
                    border: 1px solid ${CFG.THEME_PRIMARY}33;
                    border-radius: 4px;
                    color: ${CFG.THEME_TEXT_PRIMARY};
                    cursor: pointer;
                    font-size: 9px;
                    font-family: 'Courier New', monospace;
                    text-align: left;
                    transition: border-color 0.2s;
                }
                .ctrl-btn:hover      { border-color: ${CFG.THEME_PRIMARY}; }
                .ctrl-btn-warn       { border-color: ${CFG.THEME_SECONDARY}44; }
                .ctrl-btn-warn:hover { border-color: ${CFG.THEME_SECONDARY}; color: ${CFG.THEME_SECONDARY}; }
                .ctrl-btn-danger       { border-color: ${CFG.THEME_PUT}44; }
                .ctrl-btn-danger:hover { border-color: ${CFG.THEME_PUT}; color: ${CFG.THEME_PUT}; }
                .ctrl-msg {
                    font-size: 9px;
                    min-height: 14px;
                    text-align: center;
                    transition: color 0.3s;
                }
            `;
            document.head.appendChild(style);
        },

        _bindEvents() {
            const binds = [
                ['ctrl-exp-signals', () => this.exportSignalsCSV()],
                ['ctrl-exp-market',  () => this.exportMarketLogCSV()],
                ['ctrl-clr-signals', () => this.clearSignals()],
                ['ctrl-clr-market',  () => this.clearMarketLog()],
                ['ctrl-rst-session', () => this.resetSession()],
                ['ctrl-rst-all',     () => this.resetAll()],
                ['ctrl-notif',       () => this.requestNotifications()],
            ];
            binds.forEach(([id, fn]) => {
                const el = DOMUtils.ge(id);
                if (el) el.addEventListener('click', fn);
            });
        },

        _showConfirmation(msg) { return window.confirm(msg); },

        _showSuccess(msg) {
            const el = DOMUtils.ge('ctrl-msg');
            if (el) { el.textContent = `✅ ${msg}`; el.style.color = CFG.THEME_CALL; }
            setTimeout(() => { if (el) el.textContent = ''; }, 3000);
        },

        _showError(msg) {
            const el = DOMUtils.ge('ctrl-msg');
            if (el) { el.textContent = `❌ ${msg}`; el.style.color = CFG.THEME_PUT; }
            setTimeout(() => { if (el) el.textContent = ''; }, 4000);
        },

        _triggerDownload(content, filename) {
            const blob = new Blob([content], { type: 'text/csv;charset=utf-8;' });
            const url  = URL.createObjectURL(blob);
            const a    = document.createElement('a');
            a.href     = url;
            a.download = filename;
            a.click();
            URL.revokeObjectURL(url);
        },
    };

    // ════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
    // EXPOSICIÓN TEMPORAL — BLOQUE 8
    // ════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════

    window._TCP_UI_MainPanel     = UI_MainPanel;
    window._TCP_UI_Semaforo      = UI_Semaforo;
    window._TCP_UI_MultiSymbol   = UI_MultiSymbol;
    window._TCP_UI_Configuration = UI_Configuration;
    window._TCP_UI_Controls      = UI_Controls;

    console.log(`%c👑 TCP PRO v4.3 — PARTE 6 CARGADA
    ✅ [8.1] UI_MainPanel     → Panel principal draggable + Gold Theme
    ✅ [8.2] UI_Semáforo      → Shadow DOM aislado + 5 condiciones + RSI bar
    ✅ [8.3] UI_MultiSymbol   → Grid 2×2 + alertas pulsantes + toggle
    ✅ [8.4] UI_Configuration → Selectores Doji/Divergencia + validación
    ✅ [8.5] UI_Controls      → Export CSV + Reset + Notificaciones`,
        `color:${CFG.THEME_PRIMARY};font-weight:bold;font-size:12px`
    );
        // ════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
    // [BLOQUE 9.1] AntiFreeze — KEEP-ALIVE
    // ════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════

    const AntiFreeze = {

        // ─── ESTADO ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────
        _isActive:       false,
        _lastActivity:   0,
        _pingInterval:   null,
        _wakeLock:       null,
        _lockInterval:   null,

        // ─── MÉTODOS PÚBLICOS ────────────────────────────────────────────

        /**
         * Inicializar sistema anti-congelamiento
         */
        init() {
            this.activate();
            this._requestWakeLock();
            this._useNavigatorLocks();
            console.log('[AntiFreeze] ✅ Sistema anti-congelamiento iniciado.');
        },

        /**
         * Activar keep-alive
         */
        activate() {
            if (this._isActive) return;
            this._isActive     = true;
            this._lastActivity = Date.now();

            // Ping periódico al WS
            this._pingInterval = setInterval(() => {
                this.ping();
                this.checkActivity();
            }, CFG.ANTIFREEZE_PING_INTERVAL);

            console.log('[AntiFreeze] ▶️ Keep-alive activado.');
        },

        /**
         * Desactivar keep-alive
         */
        deactivate() {
            this._isActive = false;
            if (this._pingInterval) {
                clearInterval(this._pingInterval);
                this._pingInterval = null;
            }
            this._releaseWakeLock();
            console.log('[AntiFreeze] ⏹️ Keep-alive desactivado.');
        },

        /**
         * Enviar ping al WebSocket
         */
        ping() {
            this._sendPing();
            this._lastActivity = Date.now();
        },

        /**
         * Enviar heartbeat (alias de ping con log)
         */
        heartbeat() {
            this.ping();
            this._logActivity();
        },

        /**
         * Verificar actividad del sistema
         * Si lleva demasiado tiempo sin datos → alertar
         */
        checkActivity() {
            const state = window._TCP_STATE;
            if (!state) return;

            const elapsed = Date.now() - (state.wsLastMessage || 0);

            if (state.wsLastMessage > 0 &&
                elapsed > CFG.ANTIFREEZE_INACTIVIDAD_MAX) {
                console.warn(
                    `[AntiFreeze] ⚠️ Sin datos WS por ${Math.round(elapsed/1000)}s`
                );
                // Notificar al WSHealthMonitor
                if (window._TCP_WSHealthMonitor) {
                    window._TCP_WSHealthMonitor.checkHealth();
                }
            }
        },

        /**
         * Resetear sistema
         */
        reset() {
            this._lastActivity = Date.now();
            if (window._TCP_STATE) {
                window._TCP_STATE.wsLastMessage = Date.now();
            }
        },

        // ─── MÉTODOS PRIVADOS ────────────────────────────────────────────

        /**
         * Enviar ping formato Socket.IO al WS activo
         */
        _sendPing() {
            try {
                const ws = window._TCP_STATE?.ws;
                if (ws && ws.readyState === 1) {
                    ws.send('2');  // Socket.IO ping
                }
            } catch (e) { /* WS puede estar cerrado */ }
        },

        /**
         * Simular actividad del usuario (mouse move virtual)
         */
        _simulateActivity() {
            try {
                const evt = new MouseEvent('mousemove', {
                    bubbles:   true,
                    cancelable:true,
                    view:      window,
                    clientX:   Math.floor(Math.random() * window.innerWidth),
                    clientY:   Math.floor(Math.random() * window.innerHeight),
                });
                document.dispatchEvent(evt);
            } catch (e) { /* Silenciar */ }
        },

        /**
         * Solicitar Wake Lock API (mantiene pantalla activa)
         */
        async _requestWakeLock() {
            try {
                if ('wakeLock' in navigator) {
                    this._wakeLock = await navigator.wakeLock.request('screen');
                    console.log('[AntiFreeze] 🔒 Wake Lock adquirido.');

                    // Re-adquirir si se pierde (ej: tab oculta)
                    this._wakeLock.addEventListener('release', () => {
                        console.log('[AntiFreeze] 🔓 Wake Lock liberado — reintentando...');
                        setTimeout(() => this._requestWakeLock(), 2000);
                    });
                }
            } catch (e) {
                // Wake Lock no disponible (HTTP, permisos, etc.)
            }
        },

        /**
         * Liberar Wake Lock
         */
        async _releaseWakeLock() {
            try {
                if (this._wakeLock) {
                    await this._wakeLock.release();
                    this._wakeLock = null;
                }
            } catch (e) { /* Silenciar */ }
        },

        /**
         * Usar Navigator Locks API para mantener proceso activo
         */
        _useNavigatorLocks() {
            try {
                if ('locks' in navigator) {
                    navigator.locks.request(
                        'tcp_pro_keepalive',
                        { mode: 'shared' },
                        () => new Promise(() => {})  // Promise infinita = lock permanente
                    );
                    console.log('[AntiFreeze] 🔐 Navigator Lock adquirido.');
                }
            } catch (e) { /* Silenciar */ }
        },

        /**
         * Registrar actividad en consola (debug silencioso)
         */
        _logActivity() {
